```
336	    if (utrRef.trim()) updateData.utr_number = utrRef.trim()
337	    await Promise.all(
338	      screen.orderIds.map((id) =>
339	        supabase.from('orders').update(updateData).eq('id', id)
340	      )
341	    )
342	    clearFarmer(screen.farmerId)
343	    localStorage.removeItem(UPI_PENDING_KEY)
344	    localStorage.removeItem(UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY)
345	    setSubmittingResult(false)
346	    setPaidDone(true)
347	  }, [clearFarmer])
348	
349	  // Keep a ref to triggerPostPayment so visibilitychange handler is never stale
350	  const triggerPostPaymentRef = useRef(triggerPostPayment)
351	  useEffect(() => { triggerPostPaymentRef.current = triggerPostPayment }, [triggerPostPayment])
352	
353	  // On mount: only restore payment screen when UPI app was actually launched.
354	  // If launched is missing the user just viewed (or closed) the payment screen — don't restore.
355	  useEffect(() => {
356	    const pending = localStorage.getItem(UPI_PENDING_KEY)
357	    const launched = localStorage.getItem(UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY)
358	    if (!pending || !launched) return
359	    try {
360	      const saved = JSON.parse(pending) as UpiPaymentState
361	      setUpiScreen(saved)
362	      if (!autoTriggeredRef.current) {
363	        autoTriggeredRef.current = true
364	        triggerPostPayment(saved, '')
365	      }
366	    } catch { /* ignore corrupt data */ }
367	  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
368	  }, []) // intentionally runs once on mount only
369	
370	  // Persist upiScreen to localStorage whenever it changes
371	  useEffect(() => {
372	    if (upiScreen) localStorage.setItem(UPI_PENDING_KEY, JSON.stringify(upiScreen))
373	  }, [upiScreen])
374	
375	  // When user returns from UPI app (no page reload): auto-trigger WhatsApp
376	  useEffect(() => {
```

> AGENT

The restore is gated on `UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY` (only set during a real UPI app launch), so reusing `upiScreen` for COD won't wrongly restore a payment screen on reload. I'll wire COD into the success screen and just guard the persist effect so a COD success doesn't leave a stale UPI key. Here's the COD fix:

(pickupByFarmer[f.farmerId] || undefined) : undefined,\n    })\n    setPaidDone(true)\n    clearFarmer(f.farmerId)\n  }"

A COD success reuses upiScreen for its confirmation screen and\n  // has nothing to resume, so we never persist it.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (upiScreen && !cashMode) localStorage.setItem(UPI_PENDING_KEY, JSON.stringify(upiScreen))\n  }, [upiScreen, cashMode])"

> AGENT

Fixed and committed.

## What was wrong
When you clicked **Place order** (Cash on Delivery), the code placed the order and cleared the cart — but it **never showed a confirmation**. So the cart just emptied and you landed on the "Your cart is empty" screen, making it look like nothing happened.

The UPI and online-payment flows *did* set up the success screen; only the **COD path skipped it**.

## The fix
COD now shows the **same "Order placed!" success screen** the other payment methods use — with the **pop + ring animation** I added earlier:
- 💵 icon pops in with an expanding ring
- "Order placed!" title rises in
- shows the farmer, amount, and a "Check order status" link + "Done" button

I also made sure a COD confirmation doesn't get mistakenly saved as a resumable UPI payment (so reloading won't pop up a payment screen for a cash order).

Build is clean. To see it: add an item, choose **Cash on Delivery**, and tap **Place order** — you'll now get the animated confirmation instead of the empty cart.

Latest commit on `recover/order-tracking-and-reviews`:
```
157f773 fix(checkout): show animated success screen on Cash-on-Delivery orders
```

Want to test it in the browser now, or should I keep going? (Still not pushing until you give the word.)

> AGENT

Done — the buyer-cancel and farmer-decline confirmations now both have the same animated icon. Committed as `3c54c23`.

Now, your question about the **Pending orders card**. I agree with your instinct — here's my take:

The Pending card currently does two jobs at once, and there's already overlap on the home screen:
- the **⚠️ banner** ("N orders need your response") → jumps straight to *pending*
- the **Pending stat card** → also jumps to *pending*
- a separate **Orders card** lower down → opens *all* orders

So *pending* has two shortcuts while *all orders* is buried at the bottom. My recommendation: **keep the pending count** on the card (it's the key "needs action" number), but make tapping it open the **full Orders page** — where "Pending" is the very first filter chip anyway, and the urgent banner still gives the one-tap pending jump. That way the prominent card becomes your gateway to *everything*, not just pending.

Let me make that change:

> AGENT

Done. Here's where things landed.

## Your question — yes, you're right
The home screen had **two shortcuts to pending** (the ⚠️ banner *and* the Pending card) but **all-orders access was buried** at the bottom. So I changed it the way I recommended:

- The **Pending card** still shows the **pending count** (your key "needs action" number)…
- …but tapping it now opens the **full Orders page** (where "Pending" is the first filter chip, one tap away).
- The **⚠️ banner** still gives the direct one-tap jump to pending when something's waiting.

So the prominent card is now your gateway to **all orders**, not just pending — exactly your point.

Committed as `bd2dd9f`.

## Everything from this session (branch `recover/order-tracking-and-reviews`)

Otherwise — good moment to test in the browser? And the branch still isn't pushed; say the word whenever you want it backed up to the remote.

> AGENT

That one's on me — when I ran `rm -rf .next` for the clean type-check earlier, your **dev server was running at the same time**, so I deleted its build cache out from under it. Turbopack's on-disk cache got half-wiped (the missing `.sst`/`build-manifest.json` files), and it can't recover mid-run. Nothing wrong with your code — it's purely the dev cache.

It's a 30-second fix. In the terminal where the dev server is running:

1. **Stop it** — `Ctrl+C`
2. **Clear the cache and restart:**

Type this in your prompt (the `!` runs it in your session):

```
! rm -rf .next && npm run dev
```

That rebuilds a fresh cache and the server will come up clean.

Going forward I'll **never delete `.next` while your dev server is up** — if I need a clean type-check I'll use a separate build directory instead so it can't touch your running server.

Want me to verify the code is fine by running a production build in an isolated output dir (won't touch your dev server), or are you good to just restart?

```
+  // Farmer sets/updates the pickup-or-delivery date on an order.
+  const handleSetFulfillmentDate = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
+    const value = date || null
+    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, fulfillment_date: value } : o)))
+    await supabase.from('orders').update({ fulfillment_date: value }).eq('id', orderId)
+  }
+
+  // Approving requires a pickup/delivery date. Guard on status='pending' so a
+  // buyer-cancelled (or declined) order can never be resurrected into 'approved'.
+  const handleApprove = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
+    if (!date) return
+    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
+    const { data, error } = await supabase
+      .from('orders')
+      .update({ status: 'approved', fulfillment_date: date, confirmed_at: new Date().toISOString() })
+      .eq('id', orderId)
+      .eq('status', 'pending')
+      .select('id')
+    if (error || !data?.length) {
+      await loadDashboard()
+      setProcessingOrderId(null)
+      alert(L('This order can no longer be approved — the buyer may have cancelled it.', 'ఈ ఆర్డర్‌ను ఇప్పుడు ఆమోదించలేరు — కొనుగోలుదారు రద్దు చేసి ఉండవచ్చు.'))
+      return
+    }
+    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
+      prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, status: 'approved', fulfillment_date: date } : o)),
+    )
+    setProcessingOrderId(null)
+  }
+
```

```
=== pickup-slots lib ===
// A farmer's pickup schedule is keyed BY pickup location: each location has its
// own list of windows, each window its own set of weekdays plus a from/to time —
// e.g. "Bus stand" → Mon/Wed/Fri 08:00–12:00 AND Sat/Sun 16:00–18:00, while
// "Market road" → Sun 17:00–19:00. Stored in the farmers.pickup_slots JSON column
// as { [locationName]: PickupSlot[] }.

export type PickupSlot = {
  days: string[]
  time_from: string
  time_to: string
}

// Per-location pickup schedule: location name → its time windows.
export type PickupSchedule = Record<string, PickupSlot[]>

export const PICKUP_DEFAULT_FROM = '08:00'
export const PICKUP_DEFAULT_TO = '12:00'

// Normalize the raw pickup_slots value into a clean array of slots. Accepts
// either the new array shape, the LEGACY single-object shape
// ({ days, time_from, time_to }) saved before multi-slot support, or null —
// always returns an array. Slots with no chosen days are dropped, so callers
// can persist `result.length ? result : null`.
export function normalizePickupSlots(raw: unknown): PickupSlot[] {
  const list = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : raw ? [raw] : []
  const out: PickupSlot[] = []
  for (const s of list) {
    if (!s || typeof s !== 'object') continue
    const o = s as { days?: unknown; time_from?: unknown; time_to?: unknown }
    const days = Array.isArray(o.days)
      ? Array.from(new Set(o.days.map((d) => String(d)).filter(Boolean)))
      : []
    if (days.length === 0) continue
    out.push({
      days,
      time_from: typeof o.time_from === 'string' && o.time_from ? o.time_from : PICKUP_DEFAULT_FROM,
      time_to: typeof o.time_to === 'string' && o.time_to ? o.time_to : PICKUP_DEFAULT_TO,
    })
  }
  return out
```

```
79:export function normalizePickupSchedule(raw: unknown, locations?: string[]): PickupSchedule {
export function normalizePickupSchedule(raw: unknown, locations?: string[]): PickupSchedule {
  const out: PickupSchedule = {}

  if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
    // Legacy global schedule — attach to the first known location, if any.
    const slots = normalizePickupSlots(raw)
    const first = locations?.[0]
    if (slots.length > 0 && first) out[first] = slots
    return out
  }

  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') {
    const map = raw as Record<string, unknown>
    const keys = locations ?? Object.keys(map)
    for (const loc of keys) {
      const slots = normalizePickupSlots(map[loc])
      if (slots.length > 0) out[loc] = slots
    }
  }

  return out
}
```

Example: yourname@ybl or 9876543210@paytm')\n      return\n    }\n    // Pickup location + timing is mandatory: a buyer choosing self-pickup must\n    // always know WHERE and WHEN to collect, so every farmer needs at least one\n    // pickup point and a timing window for each.\n    if (pickupLocations.length === 0) {\n      setError(L('Add at least one pickup location so buyers know where to collect their order.', 'కొనుగోలుదారులు ఆర్డర్ ఎక్కడ తీసుకోవాలో తెలియడానికి కనీసం ఒక పికప్ స్థలాన్ని జోడించండి.'))\n      return\n    }\n    const cleanSlots = normalizePickupSchedule(schedule, pickupLocations)\n    const missingTiming = pickupLocations.filter((loc) => !(cleanSlots[loc]?.length))\n    if (missingTiming.length > 0) {\n      setError(L(`Add pickup timings for: ${missingTiming.join(', ')}`, `వీటికి పికప్ సమయాలను జోడించండి: ${missingTiming.join(', ')}`))\n      return\n    }\n    setLoading(true)"
```json
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Two-part fix for the self-pickup issue:

## 1. Farmers must now set a pickup location + timing (the real fix)
In the farmer profile editor's save (`dashboard/page.tsx`), saving is now **blocked** unless:
- there's **at least one pickup location**, and
- **every** location has at least one **timing window**.

Clear error messages guide them (e.g. *"Add at least one pickup location…"* / *"Add pickup timings for: …"*). So going forward, no farmer can go live without telling buyers where and when to collect.

## 2. The cart always shows a pickup location now (immediate fix + safety net)
Previously, if a farmer hadn't set pickup points, the self-pickup option rendered **nothing** — exactly what you saw. Now, in that case the cart shows a fallback: 📍 the farm's **village** + *"The farmer will confirm the exact pickup point and timing on WhatsApp."* This covers existing farmers/orders until they update their profile.

✅ **Type-check passes** (used `tsc`, so `.next` and your dev server were untouched).

## Commit status
- **Committed:** `104911b` — the cart fallback (clean, mine).
- **NOT committed:** the farmer-mandatory validation in `dashboard/page.tsx` — that file still holds **your** in-progress edits (order handlers + cards you're adding to home), so I left the whole file uncommitted rather than bundle your WIP. My pickup validation is in there alongside your work, ready whenever you want to commit.

```
11	export default function GlobalNav({ activeTab = 'consumer' }: { activeTab?: ActiveTab }) {
12	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
13	  const { state, consumer, openAuth, logout, suspendedReason, dismissSuspension } = useConsumerAuth()
14	
15	  const tabs = [
16	    { key: 'consumer' as const, href: '/consumer', label: tx.consumerNav },
17	    { key: 'farmer' as const, href: '/farmer/dashboard', label: tx.farmerNav },
18	    { key: 'delivery' as const, href: '/rider', label: tx.deliveryNav },
19	    { key: 'moderator' as const, href: '#', label: tx.moderatorNav, disabled: true },
20	  ]
21	
22	  return (
23	    <nav className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-green-900 shadow-lg">
24	      {/* Logo row */}
25	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-green-800 gap-2">
26	        <Link href="/consumer" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
27	          <div className="w-9 h-9 bg-green-700 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
28	            <span className="text-white font-black text-xs">YFF</span>
29	          </div>
30	          <div className="leading-tight min-w-0">
31	            <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm block truncate">{L('YourFamilyFarmer', 'యువర్ ఫ్యామిలీ ఫార్మర్')}</span>
32	          </div>
33	        </Link>
34	
35	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 flex-shrink-0">
36	          <Link
37	            href="/consumer/complaints"
38	            aria-label="Log a Complaint"
39	            title={L('Log a Complaint', 'ఫిర్యాదు')}
40	            className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-950 bg-amber-400 active:bg-amber-500 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight whitespace-nowrap"
41	          >
42	            🛟 Complaint
43	          </Link>
44	          {state.status === 'loading' ? null : consumer ? (
45	            <ConsumerMenu name={consumer.name} onLogout={logout} />
46	          ) : (
47	            <button
48	              onClick={openAuth}
49	              className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-100 bg-green-800 active:bg-green-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight"
50	            >
51	              {L('Login', 'లాగిన్')}
52	            </button>
53	          )}
54	          <LanguageToggle />
55	        </div>
56	      </div>
57	
58	      {/* Role tabs */}
59	      <div className="flex">
60	        {tabs.map((tab) => {
61	          const isActive = tab.key === activeTab
62	
63	          if (tab.disabled) {
64	            return (
65	              <span key={tab.key} className="flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-medium">
66	                {tab.label}
67	              </span>
68	            )
69	          }
70	
71	          return (
72	            <Link
73	              key={tab.key}
74	              href={tab.href}
75	              className={`flex-1 text-center py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
76	                isActive
77	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-b-2 border-green-300'
78	                  : 'text-green-300 hover:text-white'
79	              }`}
80	            >
81	              {tab.label}
82	            </Link>
83	          )
84	        })}
85	      </div>
86	
87	      {/* Suspension banner — shown when this account has been suspended by a
88	          moderator, surfacing the reason they gave. */}
89	      {suspendedReason && (
90	        <div className="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2.5 flex items-start gap-2.5">
91	          <span className="text-base leading-none mt-0.5" aria-hidden>🚫</span>
92	          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
93	            <p className="text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wide leading-tight">
94	              {L('Account suspended', 'ఖాతా నిలిపివేయబడింది')}
95	            </p>
96	            <p className="text-xs font-medium leading-snug mt-0.5 break-words">{suspendedReason}</p>
97	          </div>
98	          <button
99	            onClick={dismissSuspension}
100	            aria-label="Dismiss"
101	            className="text-white/80 active:text-white text-lg leading-none px-1 flex-shrink-0"
102	          >
103	            ×
104	          </button>
105	        </div>
106	      )}
107	    </nav>
108	  )
109	}
110	
111	function ConsumerMenu({ name, onLogout }: { name: string | null; onLogout: () => Promise<void> }) {
112	  const { L } = useLang()
113	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
114	  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
115	  const firstName = (name ?? '').split(' ')[0] || 'there'
116	
117	  useEffect(() => {
118	    if (!open) return
119	    const onDocClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
120	      if (!wrapRef.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)
121	    }
122	    const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false) }
123	    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)
124	    document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc)
125	    return () => {
126	      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDocClick)
127	      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc)
128	    }
129	  }, [open])
130	
131	  return (
132	    <div ref={wrapRef} className="relative">
133	      <button
134	        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
135	        aria-haspopup="menu"
136	        aria-expanded={open}
137	        className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-100 bg-green-800 active:bg-green-700 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 leading-tight max-w-[104px] truncate"
138	      >
139	        Hi {firstName} ▾
140	      </button>
141	      {open && (
142	        <div
143	          role="menu"
144	          className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-56 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 py-1.5 text-sm z-50"
145	        >
146	          <p className="px-4 py-2 text-[11px] text-gray-500 leading-tight border-b border-gray-100 mb-1">
147	            Logged in as<br />
148	            <span className="text-gray-800 font-semibold">{name || 'Consumer'}</span>
149	          </p>
150	          <Link
151	            href="/consumer/orders"
152	            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
153	            className="block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100 font-semibold"
154	          >
155	            {L('🧾 My orders', 'నా ఆర్డర్లు')}
156	          </Link>
157	          <Link
158	            href="/consumer"
159	            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
160	            className="block px-4 py-2.5 text-gray-800 active:bg-gray-100"
161	          >
162	            {L('🛒 Browse', 'కొనుగోలు')}
163	          </Link>
164	          <button
165	            onClick={() => { setOpen(false); void onLogout() }}
166	            className="block w-full text-left px-4 py-2.5 text-red-600 active:bg-red-50 font-semibold border-t border-gray-100 mt-1"
167	          >
168	            {L('↪ Log out', 'లాగౌట్')}
169	          </button>
170	        </div>
171	      )}
172	    </div>
173	  )
174	}
175	
```
